       -i /root/archhurd-build/glibc/mach/tmp_ \
       -server /dev/null -user /dev/null -header /dev/null
for call in device_open device_close device_write device_write_inband device_read device_read_inband device_map device_set_status device_get_status device_set_filter device_intr_register device_intr_ack; do \
  echo "weak_alias (__$call, $call)" >> /root/archhurd-build/glibc/mach/tmp_${call}.c; \
  /bin/sh ../scripts/move-if-change /root/archhurd-build/glibc/mach/tmp_${call}.c \
		    /root/archhurd-build/glibc/mach/RPC_${call}.c; \
done
